Featured Ads  <span class="drop_down_main"><a class="flag_drop_down">Feature Your Ad Here<span class="bbb-icon bbb-i-arrowdown"></span></a></span>
</div>
<div class="featured_main_border"> </div>
<div id="youradhere_newpost">
<div class="youradhere_heading">
Featured Ads</div>
<div class="youradhere_body">
If you'd like for your ad to be featured here, choose the "feature this ad" option while posting your local or all cities ad. Why should you feature your ad? As a rule, featured ads receive significantly more visits. Here's why:
<br/><br/>
<span class="large_bullets">•</span> Featured ads have the <span class="featured_promo">FEATURED</span> sign on them.
<br/>
<span class="large_bullets">•</span> Featured ads appear above the free / regular ads.
<br/>
<span class="large_bullets">•</span> Featured ads appear in the home page slider.
<br/>
<span class="large_bullets">•</span> Featured ads come up first in search results.
<br/>
<span class="large_bullets">•</span> Featured ads cannot be flagged for removal by users.
<br/>
<br/>
And... Featured ads do not have to be posted in the right categories. You can post your featured ad in any category you choose.
<br/><div class="youradhere_close"><a class="small_button">Close</a></div>
</div>
</div>

<h3><div class="fa_icons_small"><i class="fa fa-check"></i></div> Fast, Easy &amp; Effective</h3>
<div class="commentitem">
Fast, easy to use, effective - that's exactly what QwikAd.com is. It takes just a few seconds to post an ad on our classifieds. Post free or paid ads for jobs, pets, business and marketing, for sale, housing and so much more. Find everything from electronics to clothes, vehicles, real estate, services and business opportunities. An account is not required to post ads. So, why not post one now?
</div>

<h3><div class="fa_icons_small"><i class="fa fa-check"></i></div> Fast, Easy &amp; Effective</h3>
<div class="commentitem">
Fast, easy to use, effective - that's exactly what QwikAd.com is. It takes just a few seconds to post an ad on our classifieds. Post free or paid ads for jobs, pets, business and marketing, for sale, housing and so much more. Find everything from electronics to clothes, vehicles, real estate, services and business opportunities. An account is not required to post ads. So, why not post one now?
</div>
<h3><div class="fa_icons_small"><i class="fa fa-question"></i></div> Need Help?</h3>
<div class="commentitem">
<a href="?view=page&amp;pagename=contact">Email</a> us if you have any questions or visit our <a href="?view=page&amp;pagename=help">FAQ / Help</a> page.
</div>
